# Commands · P · part 11

1,549 entries, post-review-pivot to pr-description. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **post-review-pivot**: Capture what a PR or team review changed about the intended approach, separate keep from revert, and update task memory. Does not implement product code. Use when review changes direction (wrong fields or migrations) or a pivot digest is needed. Do not use for corrective feedback (use pr-feedback-ingest), when there is no concrete review signal yet, trivial fixes (use implement-approved-slice), memory drift (use state-reconcile or sync-task-state) or new work. ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/post-review-pivot.md) · ★10)

([schalkneethling/schalkneethling.com](https://github.com/schalkneethling/schalkneethling.com/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/post-scaffold.md) · ★3)

- **power.rs**: `set_prevent_sleep(enabled)` / `get_prevent_sleep()`. macOS: spawns `caffeinate -dims -w &lt;our pid&gt;` — `-w` ties the assertion to the app's lifetime, so a crash/quit can never leave an orphan keeping the machine awake (toggle-off kills the child early). Non-macOS returns Err so the frontend toggle stays off. Child handle lives in the managed `PreventSleepState` (registered in [[lib.rs]]). Frontend ([NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus](https://github.com/NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus/blob/HEAD/.mindflow/mirror/tauri/src-tauri/src/commands/power.rs.md) · ★91)
